SIZE: 901 bytes
####################################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	if err := Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("run migrations: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: .backups\migration-hardening-20261104-072348\internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 1723 bytes
####################################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	_, err := db.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}

	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")

		var exists bool
		err := db.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1
			)`,
			version,
		).Scan(&exists)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if exists {
			continue
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version) VALUES ($1)`,
				version,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package httpapi

import (
	"database/sql"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/health"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	h := health.Handler{DB: db}

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /healthz", h.Liveness)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /readyz", h.Readiness)

	return mux
}

FILE: cmd\migrate\main.go
SIZE: 1001 bytes

package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("invalid configuration: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
		log.Fatal("DATABASE_URL is required for migration execution")
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	db, err := database.Open(ctx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("database connection failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer db.Close()

	if err := database.Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("migration failed: %v", err)
	}

	log.Print("Database migrations completed successfully.")
	os.Exit(0)
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: cmd\server\main.go
SIZE: 1963 bytes
####################################################################################################
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/httpapi"
)

func main() {
	logger := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil))
	slog.SetDefault(logger)

	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("invalid configuration", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	startupCtx, startupCancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
	)

	db, err := database.Open(startupCtx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	startupCancel()

	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("database initialization failed", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	if db != nil {
		defer db.Close()
	}

	server := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.HTTPAddr,
		Handler:           httpapi.New(db),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	serverErrors := make(chan error, 1)

	go func() {
		logger.Info("HTTP server starting", "address", cfg.HTTPAddr)
		serverErrors <- server.ListenAndServe()
	}()

	signalCtx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(
		context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM,
	)
	defer stop()

	select {
	case err := <-serverErrors:
		if !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			logger.Error("HTTP server stopped unexpectedly", "error", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}

	case <-signalCtx.Done():
		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
			context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
		)
		defer cancel()

		if err := server.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
			logger.Error("graceful shutdown failed", "error", err)
			_ = server.Close()
		}
	}
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: config\config.go
SIZE: 1472 bytes
####################################################################################################
package config

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Config struct {
	HTTPAddr          string
	DatabaseURL       string
	DBMaxOpenConns    int
	DBMaxIdleConns    int
	DBConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Load() (Config, error) {
	cfg := Config{
		HTTPAddr:          env("HTTP_ADDR", ":8080"),
		DatabaseURL:       os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL"),
		DBMaxOpenConns:    10,
		DBMaxIdleConns:    5,
		DBConnMaxLifetime: 5 * time.Minute,
	}

	var err error

	cfg.DBMaxOpenConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNS", 10)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	cfg.DBMaxIdleConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNS", 5)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	if cfg.DBMaxOpenConns < 1 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns < 0 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns > cfg.DBMaxOpenConns {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid database connection pool settings")
	}

	if value := os.Getenv("DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME"); value != "" {
		cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime, err = time.ParseDuration(value)
		if err != nil || cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime <= 0 {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME")
		}
	}

	return cfg, nil
}

func env(key, fallback string) string {
	if value := os.Getenv(key); value != "" {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

func intEnv(key string, fallback int) (int, error) {
	value := os.Getenv(key)
	if value == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}

	n, err := strconv.Atoi(value)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid %s: %w", key, err)
	}

	return n, nil
}

# General Market Backend - Architecture Map

## Purpose

The General Market backend coordinates offers, supply requests, orders,
matching, trades, and trade execution workflows. It does not replace the
independent systems that own identity, balances, contracts, services,
ownership records, or financial settlement.

## Logical tree

Ã¢â€â€š   Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ redis/                   Optional; deferred until justified
Ã¢â€â€š   Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ messaging/               Optional; deferred until justified
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ migrations/                  Versioned database migrations
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ tests/                       Automated test strategy and test suites
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ scripts/                     Development and maintenance scripts
Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ docs/architecture/           Architecture documentation

## Systems of record

- Account: identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions.
- My Assets: free balance, buying power, reservation/release, asset positions.
- Contracts: legal contracts and obligations.
- Services: transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, delivery.
- Settlement: authoritative financial settlement execution and result.
- Official institutions: authoritative legal registration or official records,
  where applicable.
- General Market: market workflow, offers, orders, matching, trades, references,
  execution policy, and coordination states.

## Mandatory design constraints

1. The client is not authoritative for permissions, buying power, matching,
   or state transitions.
2. Financial and asset balances must not be independently duplicated here.
3. External operations require explicit contracts, timeouts, error handling,
   correlation identifiers, and safe retry behavior.
4. Concurrent order and matching operations require transactional safeguards
   and idempotency.
5. Published configuration and execution policy versions must be traceable.
6. Important transitions must be auditable without copying complete external
   records unnecessarily.
7. Database migrations must be versioned, reviewable, and non-destructive by
   default. Never rebuild or erase an existing database automatically.
8. Redis and message brokers are optional; introduce them only after a
   measured technical requirement.
9. A directory or README is not a completed feature. Each module needs code,
   API contracts where applicable, automated tests, and acceptance checks.
10. Infrastructure adapters must have a single clear location. If an older
    implementation exists under internal/database, review imports and behavior
    before consolidating or moving it to infrastructure/postgres.

## Implementation order

1. Inspect current repository, Go packages, migrations, configuration, and API.
2. Stabilize application bootstrap, PostgreSQL access, errors, logging, health,
   and test harness.
3. Implement catalogue and market/offer configuration.
4. Implement offers and eligibility.
5. Implement supply requests, orders, reservations, and matching.
6. Implement trades, state machines, execution policies, and workflows.
7. Add integrations, commodity aggregation, real-asset workflows, and audit
   coverage as required by the detailed specification.
8. Verify concurrency, security, performance, migrations, and recovery.

This document defines logical responsibilities, not separate deployable
services. Go package boundaries must remain simple and avoid circular imports.

package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	if err := Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("run migrations: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: .backups\migration-hardening-20261104-072348\internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 1723 bytes
##########################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	_, err := db.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}

	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")

		var exists bool
		err := db.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1
			)`,
			version,
		).Scan(&exists)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if exists {
			continue
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version) VALUES ($1)`,
				version,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package httpapi

import (
	"database/sql"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/health"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	h := health.Handler{DB: db}

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /healthz", h.Liveness)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /readyz", h.Readiness)

	return mux
}

FILE: cmd\migrate\main.go
SIZE: 1001 bytes
##########################################################################################
package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("invalid configuration: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
		log.Fatal("DATABASE_URL is required for migration execution")
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	db, err := database.Open(ctx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("database connection failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer db.Close()

	if err := database.Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("migration failed: %v", err)
	}

	log.Print("Database migrations completed successfully.")
	os.Exit(0)
}

package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/httpapi"
)

func main() {
	logger := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil))
	slog.SetDefault(logger)

	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("invalid configuration", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	startupCtx, startupCancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
	)

	db, err := database.Open(startupCtx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	startupCancel()

	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("database initialization failed", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	if db != nil {
		defer db.Close()
	}

	server := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.HTTPAddr,
		Handler:           httpapi.New(db),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	serverErrors := make(chan error, 1)

	go func() {
		logger.Info("HTTP server starting", "address", cfg.HTTPAddr)
		serverErrors <- server.ListenAndServe()
	}()

	signalCtx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(
		context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM,
	)
	defer stop()

	select {
	case err := <-serverErrors:
		if !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			logger.Error("HTTP server stopped unexpectedly", "error", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}

	case <-signalCtx.Done():
		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
			context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
		)
		defer cancel()

		if err := server.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
			logger.Error("graceful shutdown failed", "error", err)
			_ = server.Close()
		}
	}
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: config\config.go
SIZE: 1472 bytes
##########################################################################################
package config

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Config struct {
	HTTPAddr          string
	DatabaseURL       string
	DBMaxOpenConns    int
	DBMaxIdleConns    int
	DBConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Load() (Config, error) {
	cfg := Config{
		HTTPAddr:          env("HTTP_ADDR", ":8080"),
		DatabaseURL:       os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL"),
		DBMaxOpenConns:    10,
		DBMaxIdleConns:    5,
		DBConnMaxLifetime: 5 * time.Minute,
	}

	var err error

	cfg.DBMaxOpenConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNS", 10)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	cfg.DBMaxIdleConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNS", 5)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	if cfg.DBMaxOpenConns < 1 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns < 0 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns > cfg.DBMaxOpenConns {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid database connection pool settings")
	}

	if value := os.Getenv("DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME"); value != "" {
		cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime, err = time.ParseDuration(value)
		if err != nil || cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime <= 0 {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME")
		}
	}

	return cfg, nil
}

func env(key, fallback string) string {
	if value := os.Getenv(key); value != "" {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

func intEnv(key string, fallback int) (int, error) {
	value := os.Getenv(key)
	if value == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}

	n, err := strconv.Atoi(value)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid %s: %w", key, err)
	}

	return n, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\catalogue\handler.go
SIZE: 9233 bytes
##########################################################################################
package catalogue

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const (
	defaultPageSize = 20
	maxPageSize     = 100
	maxSearchLength = 120
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

type OfferSummary struct {
	ID                 string          `json:"id"`
	Code               string          `json:"code"`
	MarketID           string          `json:"market_id"`
	MarketName         string          `json:"market_name"`
	PublicCategoryID   string          `json:"public_category_id,omitempty"`
	PublicCategoryName string          `json:"public_category_name,omitempty"`
	ClassificationID   string          `json:"classification_id"`
	Title              string          `json:"title"`
	Summary            string          `json:"summary,omitempty"`
	OfferType          string          `json:"offer_type"`
	PriceAmount        string          `json:"price_amount,omitempty"`
	CurrencyCode       string          `json:"currency_code,omitempty"`
	QuantityTotal      string          `json:"quantity_total,omitempty"`
	QuantityUnit       string          `json:"quantity_unit,omitempty"`
	Attributes         json.RawMessage `json:"attributes"`
	PublishedAt        time.Time       `json:"published_at"`
	ValidUntil         *time.Time      `json:"valid_until,omitempty"`
}

type ListResponse struct {
	Data       []OfferSummary `json:"data"`
	Page       int            `json:"page"`
	PageSize   int            `json:"page_size"`
	Total      int64          `json:"total"`
	TotalPages int64          `json:"total_pages"`
}

type APIError struct {
	Code    string `json:"code"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (h Handler) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers", h.ListOffers)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers/{id}", h.GetOffer)
}

func (h Handler) ListOffers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	page, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page"), 1, 1000000)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page", "page must be a positive integer.")
		return
	}

	pageSize, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page_size"), defaultPageSize, maxPageSize)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page_size", "page_size must be between 1 and 100.")
		return
	}

	search := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("q"))
	if len(search) > maxSearchLength {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "search_too_long", "q must not exceed 120 characters.")
		return
	}

	categoryID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("category_id"))
	marketID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("market_id"))

	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	const filter = `
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
		  AND ($1 = '' OR o.title ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' OR COALESCE(o.summary, '') ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%')
		  AND ($2 = '' OR o.public_category_id::text = $2)
		  AND ($3 = '' OR o.market_id::text = $3)
	`

	var total int64
	countQuery := "SELECT count(*) " + filter
	if err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), countQuery, search, categoryID, marketID).Scan(&total); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
	const listSelect = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
	`
	rows, err := h.DB.QueryContext(
		r.Context(),
		listSelect+filter+`
			ORDER BY o.published_at DESC, o.id DESC
			LIMIT $4 OFFSET $5
		`,
		search, categoryID, marketID, pageSize, offset,
	)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	data := make([]OfferSummary, 0, pageSize)
	for rows.Next() {
		var item OfferSummary
		var categoryIDValue string
		var categoryName string
		var validUntil sql.NullTime
		var attributes []byte

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&item.ID,
			&item.Code,
			&item.MarketID,
			&item.MarketName,
			&categoryIDValue,
			&categoryName,
			&item.ClassificationID,
			&item.Title,
			&item.Summary,
			&item.OfferType,
			&item.PriceAmount,
			&item.CurrencyCode,
			&item.QuantityTotal,
			&item.QuantityUnit,
			&attributes,
			&item.PublishedAt,
			&validUntil,
		); err != nil {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_scan_failed", "A catalogue record could not be read.")
			return
		}

		item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
		item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
		if validUntil.Valid {
			value := validUntil.Time
			item.ValidUntil = &value
		}
		if len(attributes) == 0 {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
		} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
		} else {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "A catalogue record contains invalid attributes.")
			return
		}
		data = append(data, item)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_read_failed", "The catalogue could not be read completely.")
		return
	}

	totalPages := int64(0)
	if total > 0 {
		totalPages = (total + int64(pageSize) - 1) / int64(pageSize)
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, ListResponse{
		Data:       data,
		Page:       page,
		PageSize:   pageSize,
		Total:      total,
		TotalPages: totalPages,
	})
}

func (h Handler) GetOffer(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	id := strings.TrimSpace(r.PathValue("id"))
	if id == "" || len(id) > 64 {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_offer_id", "A valid offer ID is required.")
		return
	}

	const query = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.id::text = $1
		  AND o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
	`

	var item OfferSummary
	var categoryIDValue string
	var categoryName string
	var validUntil sql.NullTime
	var attributes []byte

	err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), query, id).Scan(
		&item.ID,
		&item.Code,
		&item.MarketID,
		&item.MarketName,
		&categoryIDValue,
		&categoryName,
		&item.ClassificationID,
		&item.Title,
		&item.Summary,
		&item.OfferType,
		&item.PriceAmount,
		&item.CurrencyCode,
		&item.QuantityTotal,
		&item.QuantityUnit,
		&attributes,
		&item.PublishedAt,
		&validUntil,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "offer_not_found", "The published offer was not found.")
			return
		}
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The offer could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
	item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
	if validUntil.Valid {
		value := validUntil.Time
		item.ValidUntil = &value
	}
	if len(attributes) == 0 {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
	} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
	} else {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "The offer contains invalid attributes.")
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, item)
}

func parsePositiveInt(raw string, fallback, maximum int) (int, error) {
	if raw == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}
	value, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
	if err != nil || value < 1 || value > maximum {
		return 0, errors.New("out of range")
	}
	return value, nil
}

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string) {
	writeJSON(w, status, APIError{Code: code, Message: message})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}

FILE: internal\database\database.go

package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	if cfg.MaxOpenConns > 0 {
		db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	}
	if cfg.MaxIdleConns >= 0 {
		db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	}
	if cfg.ConnMaxLifetime > 0 {
		db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)
	}

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 3443 bytes
##########################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

const migrationLockID int64 = 5270645408566230100

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	conn, err := db.Conn(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration connection: %w", err)
	}
	defer conn.Close()

	if _, err := conn.ExecContext(
		ctx,
		"SELECT pg_advisory_lock($1)",
		migrationLockID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration advisory lock: %w", err)
	}

	defer func() {
		unlockCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		_, _ = conn.ExecContext(
			unlockCtx,
			"SELECT pg_advisory_unlock($1)",
			migrationLockID,
		)
	}()

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			checksum TEXT,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		ALTER TABLE schema_migrations
		ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS checksum TEXT
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("upgrade migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}
	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		if strings.Contains(file, "..") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid migration path: %s", file)
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", file, err)
		}

		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")
		sum := sha256.Sum256(content)
		checksum := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])

		var stored sql.NullString
		err = conn.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT checksum FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1`,
			version,
		).Scan(&stored)

		if err == nil {
			if !stored.Valid || stored.String == "" {
				// Existing installations created before checksum support:
				// adopt the currently deployed migration as the baseline.
				_, err = conn.ExecContext(
					ctx,
					`UPDATE schema_migrations
					 SET checksum = $1
					 WHERE version = $2 AND checksum IS NULL`,
					checksum,
					version,
				)
				if err != nil {
					return fmt.Errorf("adopt legacy checksum for %s: %w", version, err)
				}
				continue
			}

			if stored.String != checksum {
				return fmt.Errorf(
					"migration checksum mismatch for %s: applied migration file was modified; restore the original file or create a new migration",
					version,
				)
			}
			continue
		}

		if err != sql.ErrNoRows {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := conn.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, checksum)
				 VALUES ($1, $2)`,
				version,
				checksum,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}


##########################################################################################

##########################################################################################
package health

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

func (h Handler) Liveness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ok",
	})
}

func (h Handler) Readiness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_not_configured",
		})
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := h.DB.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_unavailable",
		})
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ready",
	})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}

package health

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
)

func TestLiveness(t *testing.T) {
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/healthz", nil)
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()

	Handler{}.Liveness(rec, req)

	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("expected %d, got %d", http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestReadinessWithoutDatabase(t *testing.T) {
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/readyz", nil)
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()

	Handler{}.Readiness(rec, req)

	if rec.Code != http.StatusServiceUnavailable {
		t.Fatalf("expected %d, got %d", http.StatusServiceUnavailable, rec.Code)
	}
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\httpapi\router.go
SIZE: 1827 bytes
##########################################################################################
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/catalogue"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/live", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "alive",
		})
	})

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/ready", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if db == nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		ctx, cancel := timeouts(r)
		defer cancel()

		if err := db.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "ready",
		})
	})

	catalogue.Handler{DB: db}.Register(mux)

	return securityHeaders(mux)
}

func timeouts(r *http.Request) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
	return context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
}

func writeHealthJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, body any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(body)
}

func securityHeaders(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
		w.Header().Set("X-Frame-Options", "DENY")
		w.Header().Set("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}

WORKDIR /src

COPY go.mod go.sum ./
RUN go mod download

COPY . .
RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux go build -trimpath -ldflags="-s -w" -o /out/general-market ./cmd/server

EXPOSE 8080
ENTRYPOINT ["/usr/local/bin/general-market"]

# General Market Backend - Architecture Map

## Purpose

The General Market backend coordinates offers, supply requests, orders,
matching, trades, and trade execution workflows. It does not replace the
independent systems that own identity, balances, contracts, services,
ownership records, or financial settlement.

## Logical tree

Ã¢â€â€š   Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ postgres/                PostgreSQL adapter
Ã¢â€â€š   Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ redis/                   Optional; deferred until justified
Ã¢â€â€š   Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ messaging/               Optional; deferred until justified
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ migrations/                  Versioned database migrations
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ tests/                       Automated test strategy and test suites
Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ scripts/                     Development and maintenance scripts
Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ docs/architecture/           Architecture documentation

## Systems of record

- Account: identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions.
- My Assets: free balance, buying power, reservation/release, asset positions.
- Contracts: legal contracts and obligations.
- Services: transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, delivery.
- Settlement: authoritative financial settlement execution and result.
- Official institutions: authoritative legal registration or official records,
  where applicable.
- General Market: market workflow, offers, orders, matching, trades, references,
  execution policy, and coordination states.

## Mandatory design constraints

1. The client is not authoritative for permissions, buying power, matching,
   or state transitions.
2. Financial and asset balances must not be independently duplicated here.
3. External operations require explicit contracts, timeouts, error handling,
   correlation identifiers, and safe retry behavior.
4. Concurrent order and matching operations require transactional safeguards
   and idempotency.
5. Published configuration and execution policy versions must be traceable.
6. Important transitions must be auditable without copying complete external
   records unnecessarily.
7. Database migrations must be versioned, reviewable, and non-destructive by
   default. Never rebuild or erase an existing database automatically.
8. Redis and message brokers are optional; introduce them only after a
   measured technical requirement.
9. A directory or README is not a completed feature. Each module needs code,
   API contracts where applicable, automated tests, and acceptance checks.
10. Infrastructure adapters must have a single clear location. If an older
    implementation exists under internal/database, review imports and behavior
    before consolidating or moving it to infrastructure/postgres.

## Implementation order

1. Inspect current repository, Go packages, migrations, configuration, and API.
2. Stabilize application bootstrap, PostgreSQL access, errors, logging, health,
   and test harness.
3. Implement catalogue and market/offer configuration.
4. Implement offers and eligibility.
5. Implement supply requests, orders, reservations, and matching.
6. Implement trades, state machines, execution policies, and workflows.
7. Add integrations, commodity aggregation, real-asset workflows, and audit
   coverage as required by the detailed specification.
8. Verify concurrency, security, performance, migrations, and recovery.

This document defines logical responsibilities, not separate deployable
services. Go package boundaries must remain simple and avoid circular imports.

# Workflow and State Coordination

Responsibility:
- Coordinate trade execution across contracts, services, asset positions,
  and settlement.
- Track progress, failures, retries, and recoverable incomplete workflows.
- Do not replace the authoritative state of external services.

Implementation status: Not implemented.


##########################################################################################
FILE: README.md
##########################################################################################
# General Market Backend

Initial Go HTTP service for the General Market backend.

## Requirements

- Go 1.25 or newer
- PostgreSQL for database-backed readiness and migrations

## Go module mirror

The bootstrap script uses the Liara Go module mirror:
https://package-mirror.liara.ir/repository/go/

## Configuration

Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and set `DATABASE_URL`.

The application reads environment variables directly; it does not load `.env`
automatically. Do not commit credentials.

## Run

```powershell
go mod tidy
go test ./...
go vet ./...
go run ./cmd/server
```

## Health endpoints

- `GET /healthz`: process liveness
- `GET /readyz`: database readiness

The initial migration creates no business tables.

# Test Strategy

Planned coverage:
- Unit tests for business rules.
- PostgreSQL integration tests.
- Concurrency and idempotency tests.
- API, security, and failure-recovery tests.
- Performance benchmarks for critical queries and matching operations.

Tests must run against controlled test resources, not production data.

FILE: .backups\migration-hardening-20261104-072348\internal\database\database.go

####################################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	if err := Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("run migrations: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	_, err := db.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}

	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")

		var exists bool
		err := db.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1
			)`,
			version,
		).Scan(&exists)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if exists {
			continue
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version) VALUES ($1)`,
				version,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package httpapi

import (
	"database/sql"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/health"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	h := health.Handler{DB: db}

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /healthz", h.Liveness)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /readyz", h.Readiness)

	return mux
}

package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("invalid configuration: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
		log.Fatal("DATABASE_URL is required for migration execution")
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	db, err := database.Open(ctx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("database connection failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer db.Close()

	if err := database.Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("migration failed: %v", err)
	}

	log.Print("Database migrations completed successfully.")
	os.Exit(0)
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: cmd\server\main.go
SIZE: 1963 bytes
####################################################################################################
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/httpapi"
)

func main() {
	logger := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil))
	slog.SetDefault(logger)

	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("invalid configuration", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	startupCtx, startupCancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
	)

	db, err := database.Open(startupCtx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	startupCancel()

	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("database initialization failed", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	if db != nil {
		defer db.Close()
	}

	server := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.HTTPAddr,
		Handler:           httpapi.New(db),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	serverErrors := make(chan error, 1)

	go func() {
		logger.Info("HTTP server starting", "address", cfg.HTTPAddr)
		serverErrors <- server.ListenAndServe()
	}()

	signalCtx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(
		context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM,
	)
	defer stop()

	select {
	case err := <-serverErrors:
		if !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			logger.Error("HTTP server stopped unexpectedly", "error", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}

	case <-signalCtx.Done():
		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
			context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
		)
		defer cancel()

		if err := server.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
			logger.Error("graceful shutdown failed", "error", err)
			_ = server.Close()
		}
	}
}

FILE: config\config.go
SIZE: 1472 bytes
####################################################################################################
package config

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Config struct {
	HTTPAddr          string
	DatabaseURL       string
	DBMaxOpenConns    int
	DBMaxIdleConns    int
	DBConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Load() (Config, error) {
	cfg := Config{
		HTTPAddr:          env("HTTP_ADDR", ":8080"),
		DatabaseURL:       os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL"),
		DBMaxOpenConns:    10,
		DBMaxIdleConns:    5,
		DBConnMaxLifetime: 5 * time.Minute,
	}

	var err error

	cfg.DBMaxOpenConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNS", 10)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	cfg.DBMaxIdleConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNS", 5)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	if cfg.DBMaxOpenConns < 1 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns < 0 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns > cfg.DBMaxOpenConns {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid database connection pool settings")
	}

	if value := os.Getenv("DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME"); value != "" {
		cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime, err = time.ParseDuration(value)
		if err != nil || cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime <= 0 {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME")
		}
	}

	return cfg, nil
}

func env(key, fallback string) string {
	if value := os.Getenv(key); value != "" {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

func intEnv(key string, fallback int) (int, error) {
	value := os.Getenv(key)
	if value == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}

	n, err := strconv.Atoi(value)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid %s: %w", key, err)
	}

	return n, nil
}

# General Market Backend - Architecture Map

## Purpose

The General Market backend coordinates offers, supply requests, orders,
matching, trades, and trade execution workflows. It does not replace the
independent systems that own identity, balances, contracts, services,
ownership records, or financial settlement.

## Logical tree

Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ scripts/                     Development and maintenance scripts
Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ docs/architecture/           Architecture documentation

## Systems of record

- Account: identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions.
- My Assets: free balance, buying power, reservation/release, asset positions.
- Contracts: legal contracts and obligations.
- Services: transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, delivery.
- Settlement: authoritative financial settlement execution and result.
- Official institutions: authoritative legal registration or official records,
  where applicable.
- General Market: market workflow, offers, orders, matching, trades, references,
  execution policy, and coordination states.

## Mandatory design constraints

1. The client is not authoritative for permissions, buying power, matching,
   or state transitions.
2. Financial and asset balances must not be independently duplicated here.
3. External operations require explicit contracts, timeouts, error handling,
   correlation identifiers, and safe retry behavior.
4. Concurrent order and matching operations require transactional safeguards
   and idempotency.
5. Published configuration and execution policy versions must be traceable.
6. Important transitions must be auditable without copying complete external
   records unnecessarily.
7. Database migrations must be versioned, reviewable, and non-destructive by
   default. Never rebuild or erase an existing database automatically.
8. Redis and message brokers are optional; introduce them only after a
   measured technical requirement.
9. A directory or README is not a completed feature. Each module needs code,
   API contracts where applicable, automated tests, and acceptance checks.
10. Infrastructure adapters must have a single clear location. If an older
    implementation exists under internal/database, review imports and behavior
    before consolidating or moving it to infrastructure/postgres.

## Implementation order

1. Inspect current repository, Go packages, migrations, configuration, and API.
2. Stabilize application bootstrap, PostgreSQL access, errors, logging, health,
   and test harness.
3. Implement catalogue and market/offer configuration.
4. Implement offers and eligibility.
5. Implement supply requests, orders, reservations, and matching.
6. Implement trades, state machines, execution policies, and workflows.
7. Add integrations, commodity aggregation, real-asset workflows, and audit
   coverage as required by the detailed specification.
8. Verify concurrency, security, performance, migrations, and recovery.

This document defines logical responsibilities, not separate deployable
services. Go package boundaries must remain simple and avoid circular imports.

package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	if err := Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("run migrations: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: .backups\migration-hardening-20261104-072348\internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 1723 bytes
##########################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	_, err := db.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}

	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")

		var exists bool
		err := db.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1
			)`,
			version,
		).Scan(&exists)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if exists {
			continue
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version) VALUES ($1)`,
				version,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package httpapi

import (
	"database/sql"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/health"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	h := health.Handler{DB: db}

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /healthz", h.Liveness)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /readyz", h.Readiness)

	return mux
}

package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("invalid configuration: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
		log.Fatal("DATABASE_URL is required for migration execution")
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	db, err := database.Open(ctx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("database connection failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer db.Close()

	if err := database.Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("migration failed: %v", err)
	}

	log.Print("Database migrations completed successfully.")
	os.Exit(0)
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: cmd\server\main.go
SIZE: 1963 bytes
##########################################################################################
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/httpapi"
)

func main() {
	logger := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil))
	slog.SetDefault(logger)

	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("invalid configuration", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	startupCtx, startupCancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
	)

	db, err := database.Open(startupCtx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	startupCancel()

	if err != nil {
		logger.Error("database initialization failed", "error", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	if db != nil {
		defer db.Close()
	}

	server := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.HTTPAddr,
		Handler:           httpapi.New(db),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	serverErrors := make(chan error, 1)

	go func() {
		logger.Info("HTTP server starting", "address", cfg.HTTPAddr)
		serverErrors <- server.ListenAndServe()
	}()

	signalCtx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(
		context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM,
	)
	defer stop()

	select {
	case err := <-serverErrors:
		if !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			logger.Error("HTTP server stopped unexpectedly", "error", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}

	case <-signalCtx.Done():
		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
			context.Background(), 10*time.Second,
		)
		defer cancel()

		if err := server.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
			logger.Error("graceful shutdown failed", "error", err)
			_ = server.Close()
		}
	}
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: config\config.go
SIZE: 1472 bytes
##########################################################################################
package config

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"time"
)

type Config struct {
	HTTPAddr          string
	DatabaseURL       string
	DBMaxOpenConns    int
	DBMaxIdleConns    int
	DBConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Load() (Config, error) {
	cfg := Config{
		HTTPAddr:          env("HTTP_ADDR", ":8080"),
		DatabaseURL:       os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL"),
		DBMaxOpenConns:    10,
		DBMaxIdleConns:    5,
		DBConnMaxLifetime: 5 * time.Minute,
	}

	var err error

	cfg.DBMaxOpenConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_OPEN_CONNS", 10)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	cfg.DBMaxIdleConns, err = intEnv("DB_MAX_IDLE_CONNS", 5)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}

	if cfg.DBMaxOpenConns < 1 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns < 0 ||
		cfg.DBMaxIdleConns > cfg.DBMaxOpenConns {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid database connection pool settings")
	}

	if value := os.Getenv("DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME"); value != "" {
		cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime, err = time.ParseDuration(value)
		if err != nil || cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime <= 0 {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid DB_CONN_MAX_LIFETIME")
		}
	}

	return cfg, nil
}

func env(key, fallback string) string {
	if value := os.Getenv(key); value != "" {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

func intEnv(key string, fallback int) (int, error) {
	value := os.Getenv(key)
	if value == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}

	n, err := strconv.Atoi(value)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("invalid %s: %w", key, err)
	}

	return n, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\catalogue\handler.go
SIZE: 9233 bytes
##########################################################################################
package catalogue

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const (
	defaultPageSize = 20
	maxPageSize     = 100
	maxSearchLength = 120
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

type OfferSummary struct {
	ID                 string          `json:"id"`
	Code               string          `json:"code"`
	MarketID           string          `json:"market_id"`
	MarketName         string          `json:"market_name"`
	PublicCategoryID   string          `json:"public_category_id,omitempty"`
	PublicCategoryName string          `json:"public_category_name,omitempty"`
	ClassificationID   string          `json:"classification_id"`
	Title              string          `json:"title"`
	Summary            string          `json:"summary,omitempty"`
	OfferType          string          `json:"offer_type"`
	PriceAmount        string          `json:"price_amount,omitempty"`
	CurrencyCode       string          `json:"currency_code,omitempty"`
	QuantityTotal      string          `json:"quantity_total,omitempty"`
	QuantityUnit       string          `json:"quantity_unit,omitempty"`
	Attributes         json.RawMessage `json:"attributes"`
	PublishedAt        time.Time       `json:"published_at"`
	ValidUntil         *time.Time      `json:"valid_until,omitempty"`
}

type ListResponse struct {
	Data       []OfferSummary `json:"data"`
	Page       int            `json:"page"`
	PageSize   int            `json:"page_size"`
	Total      int64          `json:"total"`
	TotalPages int64          `json:"total_pages"`
}

type APIError struct {
	Code    string `json:"code"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (h Handler) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers", h.ListOffers)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers/{id}", h.GetOffer)
}

func (h Handler) ListOffers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	page, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page"), 1, 1000000)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page", "page must be a positive integer.")
		return
	}

	pageSize, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page_size"), defaultPageSize, maxPageSize)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page_size", "page_size must be between 1 and 100.")
		return
	}

	search := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("q"))
	if len(search) > maxSearchLength {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "search_too_long", "q must not exceed 120 characters.")
		return
	}

	categoryID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("category_id"))
	marketID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("market_id"))

	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	const filter = `
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
		  AND ($1 = '' OR o.title ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' OR COALESCE(o.summary, '') ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%')
		  AND ($2 = '' OR o.public_category_id::text = $2)
		  AND ($3 = '' OR o.market_id::text = $3)
	`

	var total int64
	countQuery := "SELECT count(*) " + filter
	if err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), countQuery, search, categoryID, marketID).Scan(&total); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
	const listSelect = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
	`
	rows, err := h.DB.QueryContext(
		r.Context(),
		listSelect+filter+`
			ORDER BY o.published_at DESC, o.id DESC
			LIMIT $4 OFFSET $5
		`,
		search, categoryID, marketID, pageSize, offset,
	)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	data := make([]OfferSummary, 0, pageSize)
	for rows.Next() {
		var item OfferSummary
		var categoryIDValue string
		var categoryName string
		var validUntil sql.NullTime
		var attributes []byte

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&item.ID,
			&item.Code,
			&item.MarketID,
			&item.MarketName,
			&categoryIDValue,
			&categoryName,
			&item.ClassificationID,
			&item.Title,
			&item.Summary,
			&item.OfferType,
			&item.PriceAmount,
			&item.CurrencyCode,
			&item.QuantityTotal,
			&item.QuantityUnit,
			&attributes,
			&item.PublishedAt,
			&validUntil,
		); err != nil {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_scan_failed", "A catalogue record could not be read.")
			return
		}

		item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
		item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
		if validUntil.Valid {
			value := validUntil.Time
			item.ValidUntil = &value
		}
		if len(attributes) == 0 {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
		} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
		} else {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "A catalogue record contains invalid attributes.")
			return
		}
		data = append(data, item)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_read_failed", "The catalogue could not be read completely.")
		return
	}

	totalPages := int64(0)
	if total > 0 {
		totalPages = (total + int64(pageSize) - 1) / int64(pageSize)
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, ListResponse{
		Data:       data,
		Page:       page,
		PageSize:   pageSize,
		Total:      total,
		TotalPages: totalPages,
	})
}

func (h Handler) GetOffer(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	id := strings.TrimSpace(r.PathValue("id"))
	if id == "" || len(id) > 64 {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_offer_id", "A valid offer ID is required.")
		return
	}

	const query = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.id::text = $1
		  AND o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
	`

	var item OfferSummary
	var categoryIDValue string
	var categoryName string
	var validUntil sql.NullTime
	var attributes []byte

	err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), query, id).Scan(
		&item.ID,
		&item.Code,
		&item.MarketID,
		&item.MarketName,
		&categoryIDValue,
		&categoryName,
		&item.ClassificationID,
		&item.Title,
		&item.Summary,
		&item.OfferType,
		&item.PriceAmount,
		&item.CurrencyCode,
		&item.QuantityTotal,
		&item.QuantityUnit,
		&attributes,
		&item.PublishedAt,
		&validUntil,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "offer_not_found", "The published offer was not found.")
			return
		}
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The offer could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
	item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
	if validUntil.Valid {
		value := validUntil.Time
		item.ValidUntil = &value
	}
	if len(attributes) == 0 {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
	} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
	} else {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "The offer contains invalid attributes.")
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, item)
}

func parsePositiveInt(raw string, fallback, maximum int) (int, error) {
	if raw == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}
	value, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
	if err != nil || value < 1 || value > maximum {
		return 0, errors.New("out of range")
	}
	return value, nil
}

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string) {
	writeJSON(w, status, APIError{Code: code, Message: message})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}

package catalogue

FILE: internal\database\database.go

package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	if cfg.MaxOpenConns > 0 {
		db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	}
	if cfg.MaxIdleConns >= 0 {
		db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	}
	if cfg.ConnMaxLifetime > 0 {
		db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)
	}

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 3443 bytes
##########################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

const migrationLockID int64 = 5270645408566230100

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	conn, err := db.Conn(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration connection: %w", err)
	}
	defer conn.Close()

	if _, err := conn.ExecContext(
		ctx,
		"SELECT pg_advisory_lock($1)",
		migrationLockID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration advisory lock: %w", err)
	}

	defer func() {
		unlockCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		_, _ = conn.ExecContext(
			unlockCtx,
			"SELECT pg_advisory_unlock($1)",
			migrationLockID,
		)
	}()

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			checksum TEXT,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		ALTER TABLE schema_migrations
		ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS checksum TEXT
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("upgrade migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}
	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		if strings.Contains(file, "..") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid migration path: %s", file)
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", file, err)
		}

		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")
		sum := sha256.Sum256(content)
		checksum := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])

		var stored sql.NullString
		err = conn.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT checksum FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1`,
			version,
		).Scan(&stored)

		if err == nil {
			if !stored.Valid || stored.String == "" {
				// Existing installations created before checksum support:
				// adopt the currently deployed migration as the baseline.
				_, err = conn.ExecContext(
					ctx,
					`UPDATE schema_migrations
					 SET checksum = $1
					 WHERE version = $2 AND checksum IS NULL`,
					checksum,
					version,
				)
				if err != nil {
					return fmt.Errorf("adopt legacy checksum for %s: %w", version, err)
				}
				continue
			}

			if stored.String != checksum {
				return fmt.Errorf(
					"migration checksum mismatch for %s: applied migration file was modified; restore the original file or create a new migration",
					version,
				)
			}
			continue
		}

		if err != sql.ErrNoRows {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := conn.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, checksum)
				 VALUES ($1, $2)`,
				version,
				checksum,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}


##########################################################################################
FILE: internal\health\health.go
SIZE: 1049 bytes
##########################################################################################
package health

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

func (h Handler) Liveness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ok",
	})
}

func (h Handler) Readiness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_not_configured",
		})
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := h.DB.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_unavailable",
		})
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ready",
	})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}
